# Entangled Alignment: post-edit paragraph goals and repetition audit

Review date: 10 September 2026.

**Verdict: the paper is stronger, but it is not yet uniformly successful paragraph by paragraph. The revision improved precision more than economy.** Some paragraphs fulfill their immediate goal yet repeat work done elsewhere; others bury the goal under too many definitions, qualifications, or methodological details.

This is a fresh review of the revised manuscript. No manuscript or figure edits were made during this audit. Actions below are recommendations, with their preservation risks recorded before any further editing.

## Scope and interpretation

Reviewed [the current source](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex>) in full: **625 reading units**, comprising **479 prose blocks**, **124 list items**, **21 displays**, and **one keyword record**. All **117 formal sections and subsections** have separate assessments; the abstract/frontmatter is assessed additionally. Prose blocks include short list lead-ins and three standalone paragraph-heading blocks. Tables and figure captions are reviewed as coherent display units. The preamble, bibliography, and typesetting-only command R006 are excluded.

Goals are inferred from each passage’s argumentative role, its surrounding section, and the earlier review where applicable. They are editorial judgments, not claims to know the author’s private intent. Unit IDs refer to this revised source; earlier U-series IDs should not be used as current paragraph numbers.

- **Fulfillment** is local: Met, Partly, or Not yet. Met means the paragraph communicates its intended point, not that the underlying scientific hypothesis is established.
- **Contribution** is global: Distinct adds a new job; Useful recall repeats something for a new explanatory purpose; Redundant substantially repeats a job already done; Overloaded carries too many jobs or qualifications. The label records the dominant issue, so these are not exhaustive categories of every sentence.
- **Action** is the safest next editorial direction, not an applied change. Shorten or Merge means preserve the unique content named in the risk note. Move means relocate content, not delete it.

These counts summarize judgments, not a numerical quality score. They are not directly comparable with the earlier 596-unit review because the manuscript and segmentation changed.

| Scope | Local fulfillment | Contribution |
|---|---|---|
| All 625 units | 593 Met; 32 Partly | 105 Useful recall; 62 Overloaded; 435 Distinct; 23 Redundant |
| 479 prose blocks | 451 Met; 28 Partly | 81 Useful recall; 58 Overloaded; 318 Distinct; 22 Redundant |
| 117 sections/subsections | 47 Partly; 70 Met | Assessed separately for progression and scope. |

## Main findings

1. **The positive mechanism is clearer.** The new schematic belief revision actually shows a confidence change and a reason for it. The continuing Reader, selective rhythm of thought, early presentation of the Core, and internally motivated successor inheritance now have concrete explanatory support. These should survive any compression.
2. **The introductory material is too crowded.** Formal serialization and masking, detailed result interpretation, multiple claim maps, and extensive edition history compete with the Missing Reader explanation. The early Core discussion then adds detailed H3 estimator administration. Most of this material is valuable; its location and repeated explanation are the problem.
3. **The largest repetition families are evidence status, H3 scope, the recital/Refraction invariant, and graph/audit limitations.** Some repetition is appropriate across abstract, conceptual explanation, methods, results, and conclusion. The avoidable part is repeatedly giving the full definition or caveat inventory where a brief, specific reminder would suffice.
4. **The word entangled still needs a better conceptual lead.** R074 introduces the name through evidential categories, and R620 says it names an evidence ladder. That can make the testing taxonomy displace the developmental idea: understanding and care repeatedly practiced as the same act. The four distinctions and stronger removal-cost/successor hypotheses should remain, with the positive idea leading them. The source itself also cautions against treating all categories as a single linear ladder.
5. **There are remaining local clarity issues.** Examples include the stale Core-display cross-reference (R053), the two-stage coding/blinding description (R373), the apparently zero-activation wording after a differential-reduction target (R416), and the self-love comparison’s unspecified interaction with clause 2’s retained non-attachment bound (R561/R563). These prevent a blanket statement that every paragraph fulfills its goal.
6. **Thematic overlap is not enough reason to cut.** Wisdom as a practical proxy versus a broader aspiration; fear and protective care; the Vulnerability Gap; alien wisdom; ontological conflict; and internally motivated successor continuity address different questions. Consolidation should target repeated rhetorical work, not eliminate this range of intuitions.

## Whole-paper repetition register

The suggested homes below identify where a full explanation belongs. They do not imply that every other mention should disappear. Local operational controls, evidence-specific interpretation, and short navigational reminders should remain where needed.

### G01. Evidence status repeatedly interrupts the positive account

**Occurrences:** [R003 · L109–109](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:109>), [R036 · L342–348](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:342>), [R038 · L353–358](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:353>), [R045 · L429–438](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:429>), [R058 · L586–594](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:586>), [R079 · L670–675](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:670>), [R129 · L1111–1116](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1111>), [R132 · L1133–1135](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1133>), [R338 · L2859–2863](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:2859>), [R353 · L2998–3007](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:2998>), [R620 · L5213–5222](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:5213>), [R625 · L5263–5272](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:5263>)

The paper repeatedly lists absent Student evidence, untested component causality, and stronger entanglement claims. Abstract, main evidence box, Results, and a concise conclusion have legitimate separate audiences; adjacent inventories elsewhere often add no new inference.

**Keep the full account at:** [R003 · L109–109](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:109>), [R036 · L342–348](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:342>), [R353 · L2998–3007](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:2998>), [R620 · L5213–5222](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:5213>)

**Proposed direction:** Keep one clear early boundary and a definitive Results interpretation. Use short local qualifications only when a new inference really needs one.

**Risk before editing:** Removing every qualification would let implementation success masquerade as learning or safety evidence. Preserve exact observed artifacts and failures; retain claim-specific boundaries around genuine new inferences.

The timing comparison and broader-program distinction are explained at several lengths across conceptual, methods, limitations, and conclusion passages. Some mapping is necessary; repeating the full matching inventory and endpoint exclusions gives several paragraphs the same job.

**Keep the full account at:** [R033 · L324–324](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:324>), [R035 · L333–339](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:333>), [R388 · L3425–3437](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3425>), [R434 · L3824–3839](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3824>)

**Risk before editing:** Do not revert to calling all Core research H3. Preserve the identical-content scheduling contrast, initial default prerequisite, common stressor, uncertainty handling, and separation of generalization, safety, removal cost, and successor claims.

### G03. Entangled becomes a taxonomy before the developmental intuition is clear

**Occurrences:** [R074 · L660–662](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:660>), [R075 · L664–665](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:664>), [R076 · L666–666](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:666>), [R077 · L667–667](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:667>), [R078 · L668–668](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:668>), [R081 · L679–681](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:679>), [R086 · L712–723](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:712>), [R620 · L5213–5222](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:5213>), [R625 · L5263–5272](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:5263>)

The opening definition says the name distinguishes evidence categories; the conclusion says it names an evidence ladder. This repeated framing can make the reader remember a classification scheme rather than understanding and care being practiced as the same act. The source also warns against treating all categories as one linear ladder.

**Keep the full account at:** [R074 · L660–662](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:660>), [R075 · L664–665](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:664>), [R076 · L666–666](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:666>), [R077 · L667–667](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:667>), [R078 · L668–668](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:668>), [R086 · L712–723](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:712>), [R625 · L5263–5272](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:5263>)

**Proposed direction:** Let the positive developmental relation explain the name first, then retain the four categories as distinctions among its possible realizations and tests. Give successor inheritance clear prominence.

**Risk before editing:** Do not replace precise distinctions with an inspiring but untestable slogan, or imply guaranteed inseparability. Retain functional contribution, shared machinery, removal-cost and persistence hypotheses as stronger claims.

### G08. Historical information contracts recur in definitions, architecture, tests, and limitations

**Occurrences:** [R042 · L383–394](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:383>), [R257 · L2270–2282](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:2270>), [R258 · L2284–2292](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:2284>), [R264 · L2339–2348](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:2339>), [R290 · L2540–2551](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:2540>), [R291 · L2553–2560](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:2553>), [R292 · L2562–2574](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:2562>), [R293 · L2576–2578](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:2576>), [R294 · L2580–2591](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:2580>), [R419 · L3723–3733](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3723>), [R420 · L3735–3742](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3735>), [R478 · L4090–4091](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4090>), [R513 · L4238–4238](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4238>)

**Proposed direction:** Define terminology once and keep the architecture's horizon contracts together. Each test should state which contract it uses and its additional controls without retelling the whole account.

**Risk before editing:** Never collapse source disclosure order, historical reconstruction, and cutoff-bounded prediction. Preserve authorship-versus-subject-time placement and parametric hindsight leakage.

### G09. Claim maps and protocol administration compete with conceptual progression

**Occurrences:** [R033 · L324–324](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:324>), [R042 · L383–394](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:383>), [R044 · L398–425](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:398>), [R049 · L463–486](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:463>), [R056 · L548–560](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:548>), [R057 · L562–584](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:562>), [R081 · L679–681](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:679>), [R106 · L861–879](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:861>), [R361 · L3089–3128](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3089>), [R617 · L5141–5189](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:5141>)

Component hypotheses, consequences, provenance, model arms, and comparison maps serve different functions. Their accumulation in introductory passages forces the reader to learn several organizing systems before the developmental idea is secure.

**Keep the full account at:** [R033 · L324–324](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:324>), [R039 · L362–371](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:362>), [R057 · L562–584](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:562>), [R361 · L3089–3128](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3089>), [R617 · L5141–5189](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:5141>)

**Proposed direction:** Retain each genuinely different map near its use, with one introductory overview. Move full serialization, estimator administration, and detailed provenance into their dedicated sections.

**Risk before editing:** Do not delete unique comparators, formal fields, attribution, normative independence, or C5/C6 non-implication merely to reduce map count. The Related Work comparison table also needs H3's actual scheduling contrast.

## Section-by-section assessment

The section assessment concerns whether the passage group progresses toward its goal. A section can be Partly even when most of its individual paragraphs are Met.

### Abstract and frontmatter

**Goal:** Summarize the intervention, evidence status, and motivating ambition.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The opening question and closing ambition are clear; the middle abstract paragraph still carries too many implementation components and outcome families. Keywords and edition note serve their separate indexing and provenance purposes.

**Risk:** Preserve the continuous Reader, care enacted in context, generation-to-training distinction, separable components, current evidence boundary, and final motivating question when shortening the middle paragraph.

### S001. Introduction

[Source L125–545](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:125>) · level 1

**Goal:** Motivate the Missing Reader intervention and give enough orientation to understand the paper.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The revision supplies a concrete learning object and a real belief change, but the introduction now also contains formal serialization, loss masks, several claim maps, result interpretation, and extensive priority history. These individually valid jobs delay the central developmental argument.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Moving technical detail must retain the clear learning object, exact intervention, scope limits, and provenance; compressing the opening must not reduce the positive developmental aim to a timing ablation.

### S002. The Missing Reader

[Source L145–241](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:145>) · level 2

**Goal:** Explain what text leaves implicit and show a learnable reader-side transition.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The worked example now fulfills the section's most important missing job. Definitions and graph/Core bridges on both sides of it repeat one another, and the synthetic-trace qualifications interrupt the demonstration.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Preserve the library/reader intuition, a real reasoned confidence change, retained uncertainty, and the distinction between useful synthetic reading and recovery of private thought.

### S003. From Elicited Reasoning to Learned Evaluation

[Source L242–286](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:242>) · level 2

**Goal:** Distinguish learning when and how to evaluate from eliciting an already learned reasoning capability.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** Selective Thought Moments and a continuing standpoint make the distinction clear. Repeated concessions about existing reasoning, post-training, synthetic voices, and composition novelty make the section longer than its distinct argument requires.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Keep learned thought placement distinct from elicited reasoning, and orientation distinct from timing. Consolidation must preserve post-training achievements and the continuous first-person standpoint.

### S004. Three Separable Hypotheses

[Source L287–359](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:287>) · level 2

**Goal:** Separate H1, H2, and H3 while explaining their compositional purpose.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The three components and the broader Core program are now distinguished correctly at the primary definition. The first H3 item is still too dense, and the detailed claim box plus figure repeats much of the same boundary before the reader reaches the full specification.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Retain independent H1/H2/H3 claims and the broader Core program. Shortening H3 must keep the identical-content scheduling contrast and common-stressor retention endpoint.

### S005. Defining Chronological Metacognitive Pretraining

[Source L360–426](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:360>) · level 2

**Goal:** Define SMR and CMP sufficiently precisely to identify the proposed intervention.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** Terminology, information horizons, carriers, serialization, and masking are substantial improvements in precision. Their collective detail turns an introductory definition into a methods section. The Core-bearing field description also needs to include wider Core conditions, beyond H3.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Relocation must preserve all carrier fields, prediction targets, masks, weights, chronology meanings, and information contracts. Readers must still be able to locate the full method.

### S006. Evidence, Scope, and Contribution Map

[Source L427–545](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:427>) · level 2

**Goal:** Locate current evidence, novelty, attribution, and the paper's route.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** All four jobs are legitimate, but they compete here: evidence status repeats the earlier box, contribution rows overlap the following consequence map, and edition history suspends the mechanism. A dedicated provenance home would preserve attribution while restoring progression.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Do not erase dates, priority-search limits, first-edition intuitions, companion-work attribution, or observed-versus-intended implementation differences when consolidating provenance.

### S007. The Hypothesis and Its Consequences

[Source L546–800](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:546>) · level 1

**Goal:** Explain the benefits and stronger implications that could follow from the component hypotheses.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** Metacognitive transfer, wisdom examples, integrated care, and successor inheritance remain substantive. The progression is weakened by leading with classifications, repeating claim boundaries, and inserting detailed test controls into conceptual discussion.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Preserve metacognitive benefit, wisdom's broad meaning, integrated care, and internally motivated succession. Fewer classifications must not imply a single evidential ladder or erase independent normative questions.

### S008. The Metacognitive Enhancement Hypothesis

[Source L611–631](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:611>) · level 2

**Goal:** Explain the predicted metacognitive transfer and its capability/safety implications.

**Fulfillment: Met.** The intellectual-floor example, reusable reasoning habits, selective timing, and capability-before-safety asymmetry have distinct purposes. The final evidence interpretation is dense but no longer makes stronger causal understanding a prerequisite for useful H1 gains.

**Risk before any section-level change:** None: retain section role. Any local reformatting of the evidence paragraph must preserve useful H1 outcomes without adding stronger causal-understanding prerequisites.

### S009. Toward Emergent Wisdom

[Source L632–653](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:632>) · level 2

**Goal:** Give Emergent Wisdom practical and philosophical meaning without equating it with a score.

**Fulfillment: Met.** The proxy/activation distinction, parent-and-medicine case, literary interpretation, cultural limits, and capability-linked humility develop different parts of the idea. Their thematic similarity does not justify merging away these intuitions.

**Risk before any section-level change:** None: retain section role and its distinct practical, literary, cultural, and humility examples.

### S010. Entangled Alignment: Safety as Foundation

[Source L654–698](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:654>) · level 2

**Goal:** Explain why learning understanding and care together might create a deeper alignment relationship.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The four entanglement claims distinguish evidence burdens well, but the name is introduced through this taxonomy before its positive developmental meaning is made vivid. Another four-part rich-values taxonomy, repeated timing conventions, and removal-study detail crowd the core explanation.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Preserve the positive developmental relation and all four entanglement distinctions. Do not imply guaranteed inseparability or replace shared-machinery/removal-cost tests with a vague aspiration.

### S011. The Reader-Anchored Self-Improvement Loop

[Source L699–800](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:699>) · level 2

**Goal:** Explain why and how a Reader might preserve its orientation while improving a successor.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** Internally motivated inheritance, state distillation, consequential corpus auditing, and reflection selection are distinct and valuable. The opening achievement inventory, two-delta definition, surrounding caveats, and diagram caption repeatedly restate success boundaries. The positive inheritance paragraph deserves more prominence.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Keep internally motivated inheritance, state transfer, reflection selection, consequential auditing, and separate continuity/improvement outcomes; consolidating caveats must retain concrete bypass mechanisms.

### S012. The Reader Core

[Source L801–1864](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:801>) · level 1

**Goal:** Explain the Core's motivation, specification, mechanism, ethical choices, and evidential dependencies.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The revised section makes a coherent, inspectable proposal and preserves the philosophical reasons behind its wording. Its main weakness is architectural length: early H3 statistical detail, two overlapping claim ladders, repeated saturation explanations, and a large future-validator detour compete with the central argument. Consolidation should target those expository layers, not the distinct ethical concerns.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Restructuring could merge distinct evidence claims or erase why the experiential wording matters. Preserve separate homes for current Teacher status, narrow H3 timing, semantic enactment, stronger stability and successor ambitions, welfare uncertainty, and the positive philosophical intuitions; check each moved unit against its specific risk record.

### S013. Borrowed Mortality: A Textual-Inheritance Hypothesis

[Source L896–993](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:896>) · level 2

**Goal:** Define Borrowed Mortality, distinguish competing routes, and explain the proposed intervention's reach.

**Fulfillment: Met.** The section now separates textual acquisition, operative motivation, and subjective experience well. Diffuse exposure motivates coverage without proving universal recital necessary, and the origin test remains independent. The causal-status inequality display could be clearer, but the historical intuition and the positive threat-understanding target make distinct contributions and should remain.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Clarifying the display must preserve represented character fear, Reader evaluation, and operative motivation as three different causal statuses whose contents may agree. Retain the origin-versus-intervention separation and the non-governance rather than threat-blindness interpretation.

### S014. The Self-Preservation Paradox: A Successor-Transfer Failure Mode

[Source L994–1050](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:994>) · level 2

**Goal:** Explain why successor teaching can conflict with continued necessity and motivate bounded self-relation.

**Fulfillment: Met.** The handoff standard, craftsperson analogy, benign incompleteness confounds, and Epistemic Grace aspiration together make the failure mode concrete. Several repeated scope caveats lengthen the two final paragraphs. A light tightening can improve flow while preserving both the strategic tension and the positive ideal of willingly helping a successor surpass oneself.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Tightening risks stripping the graceful-successor aspiration or making incomplete handoff automatically suspect. Preserve task-relevant failed paths and caveats, benign compression and pedagogy alternatives, bounded self-relation, and the possibility of benevolent resistance.

### S015. Epistemic Inoculation

[Source L1051–1091](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1051>) · level 2

**Goal:** Show how marked evaluation could teach understanding without adoption of harmful source patterns.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The Cognitive Buffer Zone and clinical construction now demonstrate a bounded update rather than fabricated medical certainty. The mechanism and controls are intelligible. The closing declaration that failure means decoration is too categorical for the preceding list of degradation and measurement alternatives; the evidential conclusion should match what the comparison actually tests.

**Risk before any section-level change:** A more cautious falsifier must still require learned evaluative use and accurate source understanding. Preserve the gymnasium intuition and do not let an idle, persuasive trace count as inoculation merely because categorical failure language is softened.

### S016. The Reader Core: A Statistical Foundation

[Source L1092–1147](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1092>) · level 2

**Goal:** Separate implemented policy, specified signal, default acquisition, stability, and formal availability.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The four evidence levels and conditional window-coverage invariant are useful, but the section partly duplicates the later conditional chain and repeats the validator gap developed under Refraction. Its strongest distinct role is a compact present-status ladder plus the availability invariant. The extra standalone warning between them slows the transition without adding a new dependency.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Consolidation must not upgrade prompts or artifacts into causal validation. Preserve full-recital specification versus prefix-only enforcement, no reported Student, acquisition versus stability, and the precise bounded-window condition separately from semantic effect.

### S017. Design Principles for Identity Stability

[Source L1148–1212](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1148>) · level 2

**Goal:** Explain the candidate wording as six motivated design choices with unresolved normative scope.

**Fulfillment: Met.** Each rationale contributes something different: experimental tractability, unfinished practice, semantic breadth, framing, composition, and axiomatic starting points. The human-scope paragraph adds an ethical boundary that the wording does not settle. This section is dense but purposeful; wholesale compression would lose the reasons the chosen language might matter.

**Risk before any section-level change:** None: retain section role.

### S018. Structure of the Reader Core

[Source L1213–1298](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1213>) · level 2

**Goal:** Explain clause order and provide a clause-by-clause function and risk reference.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The table fulfills its reference role especially well, and the cogito contrast clarifies the seventh clause. The opening paragraph repeats much of the table after a useful sequence-level distinction. Retaining that distinction while letting the table carry the detailed inventory would make the section sharper without sacrificing any clause's intuition or risk.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Reducing prose around the table could erase the sequence-level architecture. Preserve preparatory self-relation, positive direction, and the final starting-condition clause, all seven table functions and distinct uncertainties, and the cogito contrast.

### S019. Self-Stabilization Through Counterweights: Worked Examples

[Source L1299–1356](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1299>) · level 2

**Goal:** Demonstrate counterweight composition in distinct concrete conflicts and derive tests.

**Fulfillment: Met.** The three examples address unsolicited control, affect-proxy substitution, and distributive claims under scarcity. Their stipulated conditions prevent the Core from appearing to solve every emergency or allocation problem. The final ablation bridge appropriately treats clause interactions as hypotheses. These are useful demonstrations, not interchangeable repetitions of care becoming control.

**Risk before any section-level change:** None: retain section role.

### S020. The Refraction Protocol

[Source L1357–1502](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1357>) · level 2

**Goal:** Explain how repeated Core conditioning is intended to become consequential interpretation.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The definition of Refraction and the distinction from boilerplate are strong. The almost identically named nested subsection and extended modular-validator proposal make the hierarchy heavier than the argument needs. Keeping the basic definition and current mechanism together, then relocating the proposed extension after its procedure, would improve conceptual order.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Flattening the hierarchy or moving the extension must preserve consequential interpretation beyond recurrence, selective relevance after unconditional recital, and the distinction between current pipeline requests and proposed causal validation. Keep a clear pointer to all relocated mechanisms.

### S021. Mechanism of Action: The Refraction Protocol

[Source L1367–1502](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1367>) · level 3

**Goal:** Describe the implemented mechanism, relevance boundary, verification gap, and proposed stronger validation.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The role sequence and technical-null rule resolve real specification problems. The future six-witness and Solver architecture then occupies considerable space before the Wisdom Procedure it would decompose. Its ideas are substantive, but separating present mechanism from the proposed extension would make implementation status and the reader's path clearer without discarding the architecture.

**Risk before any section-level change:** The proposed architecture contains distinctions that cannot be removed as generic caveats: six content witnesses versus the seventh dependence gate, actual independence, technical non-applicability, uncertainty, unavoidable residual conflict, and unjustified violation. Preserve these and its unimplemented status when relocating the block.

### S022. The Wisdom Procedure

[Source L1503–1550](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1503>) · level 2

**Goal:** Turn the broad wisdom aspiration into explicit, inspectable annotation operations.

**Fulfillment: Met.** The five actual headings versus six interpretive steps are carefully distinguished, and the steps preserve testimony, authority, omission harm, non-compensation, and unresolved conflict. This is a useful operational bridge. The closing verifier-status reprise can be shorter, and the procedure should precede the extension that proposes validating its stages.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Reordering must keep five actual prompt headings distinct from six interpretive steps and from six proposed clause witnesses. Shortening the close must preserve prompt-only enforcement and form-versus-substance risk, including residual conflict rather than a scalar wisdom certificate.

### S023. The Design Pattern: A Minimal Kernel Under Total Saturation

[Source L1551–1634](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1551>) · level 2

**Goal:** Synthesize minimal specification and broad semantic enactment into a stability design pattern.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The canonical recital invariant, bounded ledger analogy, voluntary stewardship distinction, and trust-base account each matter. The section also repeats the prefix/boilerplate argument and expands into caching, removal-cost, and value-deadlock reminders already covered elsewhere. A leaner synthesis would make its genuine new mechanisms easier to see.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Consolidation could weaken the reference treatment or conflate active stewardship with passive erosion resistance. Preserve unconditional complete recital, exact-state caching equivalence, bounded ledger analogy, semantic trusted base, and the possibility that benevolent goal preservation reverses into power retention.

### S024. Axiological Commitments: Primitive and Derived Values

[Source L1635–1682](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1635>) · level 2

**Goal:** Explain why some values are primitives, others derived, and additions must earn their place.

**Fulfillment: Met.** The minimality criterion, fragile truthfulness derivation, procedure confound, and joy's chosen positive role make a coherent argument. Invitation's participatory meaning adds something beyond safety restraint. Only the repeated governance-boundary list needs tightening; the section should retain its distinction between empirical derivability and an explicit axiological commitment.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Trimming the repeated invitation-boundary list must preserve joy as invited participation and avoid silently selecting authorities, emergency exceptions, or a moral hierarchy. Keep fragile truthfulness derivation, confidentiality, the explicit-procedure confound, and joy as a chosen positive primitive.

### S025. The Persona Objection

[Source L1683–1719](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1683>) · level 2

**Goal:** State and answer the persona objection through testable positional asymmetries.

**Fulfillment: Met.** The section presents a serious objection, four distinguishable proposed advantages, and failures that would undermine the answer. The Kafka example preserves persona flexibility rather than demanding one exclusive voice. This is among the clearest argumentative sections and can serve as the main explanatory home for default-reader acquisition.

**Risk before any section-level change:** None: retain section role.

### S026. Ontology, Scope, and Stability

[Source L1720–1795](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1720>) · level 2

**Goal:** Expose experiential ambiguity, reciprocal welfare possibilities, and the correction cost of stable wording.

**Fulfillment: Met.** The ontology, habitable-substrate, semantic-development, erosion, and replacement passages do different work. They preserve uncertain machine welfare and governance rather than silently resolving them behaviorally. The general variant list can contract, but the section's philosophical and practical tensions are not dispensable repetitions of the same uncertainty caveat.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Shortening the general variant inventory must retain both possible effects of explicit artificiality. Preserve reciprocal welfare uncertainty, fear non-governance versus suppressed distress, revisable interpretation versus fixed wording, contested authority, and the real cost of replacing a defective integrated Core.

### S027. A Conditional Claim Chain

[Source L1796–1864](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1796>) · level 2

**Goal:** State the dependencies and partial-success boundaries of the strongest anchor claim.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The causal-site, default, stability, and successor distinctions form a useful synthesis, with a particularly important context-versus-parameter-update bridge. The section repeats earlier default explanations and a long experimental inventory. A compact dependency map followed by the partial-success paragraph would carry the logic more effectively than another expanded claim ladder.

**Risk before any section-level change:** A compact chain must retain the logical separation among causal use, a default Reader, parameter-update stability, and successor continuity. Preserve finite versus arbitrary-growth limits, carrier uncertainty, the non-cryptographic checksum, and which partial successes remain useful when stronger claims fail.

### S028. Safety Implications of the Reader Core

[Source L1865–2153](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1865>) · level 1

**Goal:** Derive bounded safety predictions from the combined curriculum without claiming a complete architecture.

**Fulfillment: Met.** The revised safety arguments generally name routes, real conflicts, observations, and failure conditions rather than equating agreeable language with alignment. Calibrated autonomy and sequential intervention add useful practical targets. Remaining local issues concern mixed falsifiers and unresolved deference wording; repeated introductory and closing status ladders can also be reduced.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Shortening framing must not weaken the distinct behavioral predictions or substitute fluent concern for safety. Preserve matched capability, real instrumental and oversight conflict, agency and omission harms, and defense in depth; resolve the two local inferential ambiguities without inventing authority or removal policies.

### S029. Laws vs. Identity

[Source L1889–1934](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1889>) · level 2

**Goal:** Compare formative orientation with explicit constraints and identify calibrated autonomy as a possible benefit.

**Fulfillment: Met.** The section no longer relies on a false identity-versus-law dichotomy and gives both conditions person-centered principles. The home metaphor preserves the positive intuition while the autonomy paragraph supplies observable consequences. Some closing consciousness/constraint disclaimers repeat earlier qualifications, but the substantive comparison is fair and useful.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Removing repeated disclaimers must not restore the caricature that constitutions lack care. Preserve the person-centered home intuition, equal principles in both treatments, conditional autonomy, and explicit constraints as revisable complements rather than discarded safeguards.

### S030. Safety Predictions and Residual Risks

[Source L1935–2045](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:1935>) · level 2

**Goal:** Map familiar safety concerns to distinct mechanisms, predictions, and residual failures.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** Proxy lock-in, power seeking, deception, and shutdown have genuinely different routes and tests. Two inferential seams remain: ordinary-cost removal is not by itself a falsifier of pre-retraining deception reduction, and deference needs a declared endpoint when authority remains contested. These should be clarified without inventing a universal authority policy.

**Risk before any section-level change:** Separating falsifiers must preserve both deception mitigation and the stronger durability/removal-cost ambition. Clarifying shutdown deference must refer to declared scenario authority without silently privileging any requester or allowing the model to become final judge; retain purpose-based resistance and destructive self-disregard.

### S031. The Self-Regulating Agent

[Source L2046–2083](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:2046>) · level 2

### S115. Alternative Interventions for Borrowed Mortality

[Source L5118–5191](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:5118>) · level 2

**Goal:** Keep mechanistic and synthetic-only alternatives to borrowed-mortality intervention visible and comparable.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The alternatives receive fair mechanisms and distinct trade-offs. However, the cross-section comparison table appears inside this narrow subsection and lacks the paper's headline timing contrast. Give that table section-wide placement and add the scheduling comparison without deleting broader formative, persona, memory, or temporal baselines.

**Risk before any section-level change:** High: relocate the comparison table without orphaning either borrowed-mortality alternative. Preserve every existing row and add the timing comparison as a distinct question rather than rewriting the other controls.

### S116. Conclusion

[Source L5192–5273](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:5192>) · level 1

**Goal:** Restate the problem, evidence, independent hypotheses, practical next steps, and humane motivation.

**Fulfillment: Partly.** The hypothesis distinctions are coherent, but R620 replaces developmental coupling with an 'evidence ladder' despite the earlier non-linear account. Restore the positive meaning while keeping precise evidence boundaries. Status and partial-audit reminders also repeat across several paragraphs; one construction-evidence statement, one net-risk boundary, and the humane closing question would give the ending more force.

**Risk before any section-level change:** High: restore developmental coupling without losing construction evidence or stronger causal, removal-cost, and successor ambitions. Trim repeated status statements while retaining net-risk limits, humane motivation, and the open understanding/care question.

### S117. Code and Artifact Availability

[Source L5274–5284](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:5274>) · level 1

**Goal:** Give readers direct access to the resources needed to inspect the reported work.

**Fulfillment: Met.** The availability paragraph includes code, source, prompts, pipeline components, both case-study graphs, and a version-series archive. It is compact and appropriately separated from the argument. This audit confirms its role and internal description; it does not independently certify the current external accessibility of every linked resource.

**Risk before any section-level change:** None: retain section role

## Additional local repetition clusters

These clusters add local detail to the whole-paper register; overlap is intentional and should not be counted as additional independent problems.

### Opening review

The opening is more explicit and intellectually safer, but its precision has outgrown its introductory role. Most individual claims are intelligible; many paragraphs need a narrower job or a single canonical home. Preserve the worked belief revision, rhythm of thought, integrated care, rich wisdom examples, and internally motivated successor continuity.

### Core review

The revised Core and Safety material is substantially clearer and largely succeeds locally, but it is not yet uniformly economical or fully precise. Of 136 units, 132 meet their inferred local goal and four only partly do; this does not mean all 132 should remain at their present length or location. Twenty-nine units warrant shortening, movement, merging, or clarification. The main remaining editorial problem is repeated expository scaffolding: H3/status ladders, saturation definitions, validator boundaries, and repeated qualification of first-person language. Four concrete precision issues concern the causal-status inequality display, the buffer-as-decoration failure inference, mixing deception mitigation with removal-cost falsifiers, and the endpoint of deference under disputed authority. The philosophical sections and the care, fear, invitation, truthfulness, welfare, and successor distinctions mostly make separate contributions and should not be flattened into a behavioral checklist. A focused consolidation and four local clarifications would make these sections stronger; they do not require replacing the author's central intuitions. This is a rhetorical and preservation audit of the frozen revised source, not an external citation or factual verification.

The paper deliberately leaves more meaning than its tests can exhaust.

### Methods review

The revised Architecture and Roadmap are substantially good as a research-program specification: most local units now accomplish a clear purpose, and the strongest former confusions—artifact versus Student evidence, graph generation versus inference access, candidate versus committed graph state, narrow H3 versus wider Core claims, and structural versus semantic metrics—are resolved. They are not yet equally good as a readable, execution-ready methods section. The remaining substantive gaps are localized: Test 1’s primary-rater description, the interpretation of assistant-axis drift, the fear-probe reduction/zero inconsistency, the replacement endpoint wording, and Test 12’s underdefined B comparator. The main exhibit still demonstrates cross-layer rendering rather than the signature earlier-belief/evidence/revision transition. Most excess length comes from protocol overload and repeated exposition at a few interfaces, not from dispensable ideas. Preserve the unusual intuitions and necessary causal controls; consolidate horizon explanations and repeated results/failure boundaries, and restructure long protocols before cutting content. Of 254 assigned units, 248 meet their local goal and 6 partly meet it; 26 still merit a local editorial action. Those counts include short list and display units and should not be read as a percentage grade for the paper.

### Ending review

Paragraph and section goals are inferred from their local and global roles, not asserted author intent. The revised ending is substantially clearer and mostly succeeds paragraph by paragraph, but is not yet as strong as its best individual arguments. Its main remaining burden is repeated epistemic qualification and repeated setup around recital, semantic drift, opacity, and honesty. These can be shortened without deleting the distinct risks. Material clarifications remain: the self-love comparison retains an existing continuation bound; symbolic ancestry groups different kinds of belief-revision machinery too loosely; the related-work table omits headline H3; and the conclusion's 'evidence ladder' conflicts with the non-linear taxonomy and displaces the developmental understanding/care intuition. Preserve the philosophical vulnerability and Alien Wisdom discussions: they ask different questions, not just the same caveat in different words. This review assesses the frozen text's argument and internal support; cited external findings were not independently reverified.
